# **## Comcast Cable Internet Speeds** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported an issue involving Comcast Cable Internet Speeds.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Network congestion may be reducing available bandwidth 

- Wi-Fi interference or weak signal may be affecting performance 

- Equipment may require a restart, update, or replacement 

- A local network or service-side fault may be limiting the connection 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected account, device, and service location 

2. Run a connection and speed diagnostic 

3. Check modem, router, signal, and network status 

4. Test the connection using a direct or alternate device where possible 

5. Apply the appropriate configuration, reset, or equipment fix 

6. Escalate persistent performance problems to Network Support 

7. Confirm that the connection meets the expected service level

# **## Comcast Imposed A New Usage Cap Of 300Gb That Punishes** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported an issue involving Comcast Imposed a New Usage Cap of 300GB that punishes streaming.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A device or network configuration may be incorrect 

- Customer equipment may be outdated or malfunctioning 

- A service-side technical fault may be affecting the connection 

- A software, firmware, or synchronization problem may be involved

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected device, service, and account 

2. Reproduce the reported technical problem where possible 

3. Run the relevant equipment and network diagnostics 

4. Check configuration, firmware, and synchronization status 

5. Apply the appropriate technical fix or equipment reset 

6. Escalate persistent faults to specialized technical support 

7. Test the service again and confirm the issue is resolved 

# **## Comcast Not Working And No Service To Boot** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported an issue involving Comcast not working and no service to boot.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A local service outage or network fault may be affecting connectivity 

- The modem, router, or connected equipment may have lost synchronization 

- A configuration or authentication error may have interrupted service 

- Maintenance or an upstream infrastructure issue may be in progress 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected service and customer account 

2. Check the network-status tools for a known outage 

3. Run equipment and connection diagnostics 

4. Restart or resynchronize the affected equipment when appropriate 

5. Apply the required configuration or technical correction 

6. Escalate unresolved faults to the network or field-support team 

7. Confirm stable service with the customer after restoration

# **## Download Speeds Slower Than Dial-Up But Paying For 25 Mbps** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that download speeds slower than dial-up but paying for 25 Mbps.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Network congestion may be reducing available bandwidth 

- Wi-Fi interference or weak signal may be affecting performance 

- Equipment may require a restart, update, or replacement 

- A local network or service-side fault may be limiting the connection 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected account, device, and service location 

2. Run a connection and speed diagnostic 

3. Check modem, router, signal, and network status 

4. Test the connection using a direct or alternate device where possible 

5. Apply the appropriate configuration, reset, or equipment fix 

6. Escalate persistent performance problems to Network Support 

7. Confirm that the connection meets the expected service level

# ## **Comcast cable company** 

**Complaint:** Comcast cable company 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Service configuration or equipment issue 

- Temporary technical fault 

- Account provisioning or compatibility problem 

- Network or platform-side error 

**Responsible Department:** Technical Support (primary) · Network Operations · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Collect the relevant technical symptoms and account details. 

2. Run service and equipment diagnostics. 

3. Apply the appropriate technical fix or escalate the fault. 

4. Verify that the reported issue is resolved. 

# ## **Lack of HBO Go Availability on Sony Devices with Xfinity Subscription** 

**Complaint:** Lack of HBO Go Availability on Sony Devices with Xfinity Subscription 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Device compatibility or application issue 

- Account authorization or service entitlement problem 

- Application, firmware, or configuration error 

- Temporary platform or content-service outage 

**Responsible Department:** Technical Support (primary) · Product Support · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Identify the affected device, application, and service entitlement. 

2. Check compatibility, account authorization, and known service issues. 

3. Apply the required configuration, update, or account correction. 

4. Confirm that the service works correctly on the affected device. 

# ## **Internet speed deteriation** 

**Complaint:** Internet speed deteriation 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Network congestion or temporary service interruption 

- Router, modem, or wireless configuration issue 

- Signal or line-quality problem 

- Local network or account provisioning issue 

**Responsible Department:** Technical Support (primary) · Network Operations · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Check the customer's service status and recent network events. 

2. Run basic connection, signal, and equipment diagnostics. 

3. Reset or reconfigure the affected equipment or escalate a line/network fault. 

4. Confirm service stability and expected performance with the customer.

**Complaint:** Comcast Business Internet 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Network congestion or temporary service interruption 

- Router, modem, or wireless configuration issue 

- Signal or line-quality problem 

- Local network or account provisioning issue 

**Responsible Department:** Technical Support (primary) · Network Operations · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Check the customer's service status and recent network events. 

2. Run basic connection, signal, and equipment diagnostics. 

3. Reset or reconfigure the affected equipment or escalate a line/network fault. 

4. Confirm service stability and expected performance with the customer. 

# **## Internet Performance Latency/Bandwidth Substantially Below What I'M Paying For For** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Internet performance (latency/bandwidth) substantially below what I'm paying for for weeks.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A device or network configuration may be incorrect 

- Customer equipment may be outdated or malfunctioning 

- A service-side technical fault may be affecting the connection 

- A software, firmware, or synchronization problem may be involved 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected device, service, and account 

2. Reproduce the reported technical problem where possible 

3. Run the relevant equipment and network diagnostics 

4. Check configuration, firmware, and synchronization status 

5. Apply the appropriate technical fix or equipment reset 

6. Escalate persistent faults to specialized technical support 

7. Test the service again and confirm the issue is resolved

# **## Comcast Cable Outage** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported an issue involving Comcast cable outage.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A local service outage or network fault may be affecting connectivity 

- The modem, router, or connected equipment may have lost synchronization 

- A configuration or authentication error may have interrupted service 

- Maintenance or an upstream infrastructure issue may be in progress 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected service and customer account 

2. Check the network-status tools for a known outage 

3. Run equipment and connection diagnostics 

4. Restart or resynchronize the affected equipment when appropriate 

5. Apply the required configuration or technical correction 

6. Escalate unresolved faults to the network or field-support team 

7. Confirm stable service with the customer after restoration

# **Common Causes:** 

- A device or network configuration may be incorrect 

- Customer equipment may be outdated or malfunctioning 

- A service-side technical fault may be affecting the connection 

- A software, firmware, or synchronization problem may be involved 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected device, service, and account 

2. Reproduce the reported technical problem where possible 

3. Run the relevant equipment and network diagnostics 

4. Check configuration, firmware, and synchronization status 

5. Apply the appropriate technical fix or equipment reset 

6. Escalate persistent faults to specialized technical support 

7. Test the service again and confirm the issue is resolved

# **## Slow Internet Speeds** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that Slow internet speeds.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Network congestion may be reducing available bandwidth 

- Wi-Fi interference or weak signal may be affecting performance 

- Equipment may require a restart, update, or replacement 

- A local network or service-side fault may be limiting the connection 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected account, device, and service location 

2. Run a connection and speed diagnostic 

3. Check modem, router, signal, and network status 

4. Test the connection using a direct or alternate device where possible 

5. Apply the appropriate configuration, reset, or equipment fix 

6. Escalate persistent performance problems to Network Support 

7. Confirm that the connection meets the expected service level

# **## Internet Not Working** 

# **Complaint: Customer reported that internet not working.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- A local service outage or network fault may be affecting connectivity 

- The modem, router, or connected equipment may have lost synchronization 

- A configuration or authentication error may have interrupted service 

- Maintenance or an upstream infrastructure issue may be in progress 

# **Responsible Department: Technical** 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Verify the affected service and customer account 

2. Check the network-status tools for a known outage 

3. Run equipment and connection diagnostics 

4. Restart or resynchronize the affected equipment when appropriate 

5. Apply the required configuration or technical correction 

6. Escalate unresolved faults to the network or field-support team 

7. Confirm stable service with the customer after restoration

# **## My internet has been down for three days now.** 

# **Complaint: My internet has been down for three days now.** 

# **Common Causes:** 

- Service configuration or equipment issue 

- Temporary technical fault 

- Account provisioning or compatibility problem 

- Network or platform-side error 

**Responsible Department:** Technical Support (primary) · Network Operations · Customer Service 

# **Resolution Steps:** 

1. Collect the relevant technical symptoms and account details. 

2. Run service and equipment diagnostics. 

3. Apply the appropriate technical fix or escalate the fault. 

4. Verify that the reported issue is resolved.
